  • 请提供或指明可取得的悦享年卡、家庭季卡权益说明，并取得其关于「生效日当天、计划结束日当天、提前终止日当天是否具有资格」的独立业务依据；在依据到手前，不以任何一方的推断代替。
  • 请就「计划结束日与提前终止日同时存在时实际有效范围如何确定」提供独立业务依据（权益说明条文、运营口径文件或经确认的记录样例）。学员此前提出的「判定日须早于两个结束日」仅属未获确认的设想，未获确认不等于错误或禁止采用，是否支持任何具体算法取决于业务依据。
  • 陈姐陈述的门店办卡用卡日期规则，本期总部运营月报是否采用？若采用，适用条件和书面依据是什么；若不采用，月报应按哪套口径、由哪一方书面确认？
  • 请运营就「门店语境下的账户级判断（任一符合范围且判定日有效的卡即形成资格、不重复计人、不重复累计订单）」是否原样适用于本期总部月报给出明确答复与依据。
  • 请提供悦享年卡、家庭季卡对应账户的开卡与终止记录样本，重点包含月底边界与两个结束日同时存在的记录；如暂时无法提供，请说明可用替代材料与预计时间。
  • 请王经理或项目组明确：本阶段正式验收由哪个组织、哪位角色有权确认，审批人与授权额度分别由谁核定；若属另行会议确定，请先书面列出待定清单。
  • 请确认阶段责任草案的定稿路径：由谁起草、哪一步进入正式评审、未决事项在定稿时如何处理；当前结构获认可不视为条款确认。
  • 请补齐费用依据：各工作项的预计人天与单价来源、费用承担方、模型调用与运行环境的计费依据与使用量口径。
  • 请补齐业务效果记录方案：记录对象、方法、负责人、观察周期，以及在何种条件下才具备基线（当前不承诺收益目标）。
  • 体验卡本期排除已有小林 MSG-04152 依据，进一步业务原因未知、可后补；如需补充，应由哪一方提供依据？
  • 请核查陈姐 MSG-04164 中尚未正式核查的两点：提前终止字段为空的实际含义，以及「本期测试月报不改变现有考核」是否可作为阶段边界陈述采用。
  • 通用指标「按门店」是否需要统一默认解释（成交门店或会员归属门店）？是否需要向小林确认；本期是否可继续使用明确问句规避默认解释。
  • 生产库、旧 POS 与跨系统自然人身份关系及真实重叠比例：本期是否需要纳入核查范围；若本期仅按测试库执行，是否在后续扩展阶段再单独核查（不在本轮重复确认材料 03 已明确的本期月内购买与复购口径）。

两份文档与下一条视频

docs/w03/business_model.md
docs/w03/interview_notes.md

当前业务模型交给 V06 验证 Agent 是否实际采用；新材料与待核实问题接续下一轮提取。

学员接下来做什么

换成自己的访谈材料和调查问题，调整目标与必要任务要求，实际运行；逐项核查并解释取舍，再确认模型修改。

课程工具负责通用读取、调用、检查和留存；业务判断由学员完成。

请求怎样组成

platform/fde_product/extraction_workflow.py · 本次运行的源码快照

122def start(ws,task_path=DEFAULT_TASK,previous=None):
123    task,config,transcripts=inspect_task(ws,task_path)
124    previous_context=None
125    if previous:
126        prior=load_session(ws,previous)
127        require(prior["status"]=="completed","PREVIOUS_ROUND_INCOMPLETE")
128        require(prior["scope_id"]==config["scope_id"],"SCOPE_MISMATCH")
129        previous_context={"session":previous,"next_questions":prior["proposal"]["next_questions"]}
130    folder="artifacts/w02/workflows/WF-"+uuid4().hex
131    snapshots={}
132    for label,path in [("task",task_path),("skill",task["skill"]),("business_model",task["business_model"]),("notes",task["notes"]),
133                       ("extraction_config",task["extraction_config"]),("prompt",config["prompt"])]:
134        dest=folder+"/inputs/"+label+(".json" if label in ("task","extraction_config") else ".md")
135        snapshots[label]=ws.write(dest,ws.raw(path),raw=True)
136    snapshots["sources"]=[ws.write(folder+"/sources/"+str(i)+".json",ws.raw(p),raw=True) for i,p in enumerate(config["sources"])]
137    impl=[ws.write(folder+"/implementation/"+p,(ROOT/p).read_bytes(),raw=True) for p in IMPLEMENTATION]
138    frozen={**config,"sources":[x["path"] for x in snapshots["sources"]],"prompt":snapshots["prompt"]["path"]}
139    ws.write(folder+"/extraction.json",frozen)
140    context={"goal":task["goal"],"previous_round":previous_context,"instruction":"请开始本轮任务，先读取材料与现有认识。"}
141    session={"version":VERSION,"folder":folder,"status":"active","task":task,"scope_id":config["scope_id"],"profile":config["profile"],
142        "started_at":datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),"previous":previous,"snapshots":snapshots,"implementation":impl,
143        "base_hashes":{task[k]:digest(ws.raw(task[k])) for k in ("business_model","notes")},
144        "messages":[{"role":"system","content":GUARD+"\n\n已加载 Skill：\n"+text(ws,snapshots["skill"]["path"])},
145                    {"role":"user","content":json.dumps(context,ensure_ascii=False)}],
146        "steps":0,"calls":[],"tool_events":[],"human_reviews":[],"reviewed":[],"shown":[],"proposal":None,
147        "extraction_run":None,"pending":None,"error":None}
148    save(ws,folder+"/session.json",session)
149    save(ws,"artifacts/w02/workflow_current.json",{"session":folder+"/session.json"})
150    return folder+"/session.json"
151

153def resolve_session(ws,path="latest"):
154    return ws.load("artifacts/w02/workflow_current.json")["session"] if path=="latest" else path
155
156
157def interrupted(s):
158    """True when the last tool calls were saved without their tool replies."""
159    for i in range(len(s["messages"])-1,-1,-1):
160        m=s["messages"][i]
161        if m.get("role")=="assistant":
162            calls=m.get("tool_calls") or []
163            return len(calls)>sum(1 for x in s["messages"][i+1:] if x.get("role")=="tool")
164    return False
165
166
167# The model never answered and the transcript is whole: the same conversation can go on after the outage.
168RETRYABLE_MODEL_ERRORS = re.compile(r"MODEL_(NETWORK_ERROR|TIMEOUT|TURN_TIMEOUT|HTTP_429|HTTP_5[0-9][0-9])")
169
170
171def can_resume(s):
172    """Judged from saved fields only, so sessions that failed under an earlier package qualify too."""
173    return (s["status"]=="failed" and isinstance(s.get("error"),str) and bool(RETRYABLE_MODEL_ERRORS.fullmatch(s["error"]))
174        and bool(s["calls"]) and s["calls"][-1].get("response") is None and not interrupted(s))

原文与人工核查

platform/fde_product/extraction_workflow.py · 本次运行的源码快照

194def tool(ws,s,name,args,extractor):
195    task=s["task"]
196    if name=="inspect_materials":
197        s["inspected"]=True
198        return {"goal":task["goal"],"business_model":text(ws,s["snapshots"]["business_model"]["path"]),
199                "notes":text(ws,s["snapshots"]["notes"]["path"]),
200                "materials":[ws.verify(ref) for ref in s["snapshots"]["sources"]],
201                "extraction_config":ws.load(s["folder"]+"/extraction.json")}
202    if name=="extract_candidates":
203        require(s.get("inspected"),"INSPECT_FIRST")
204        require(not s["extraction_run"],"ALREADY_EXTRACTED")
205        path=extract(ws,s["folder"]+"/extraction.json",extractor)
206        s["extraction_run"]=path;run=ws.load(path)
207        require(run["status"]=="succeeded",run["error"]["code"] if run["error"] else "EXTRACTION_FAILED")
208        f,_=facts(ws,s)
209        return {"run":path,"claims":f["claims"],"conflicts":f["conflicts"],"semantic_review":"required"}
210    f,path=facts(ws,s)
211    ids={c["id"] for c in f["claims"]};selected=set(args["claim_ids"])
212    require(selected<=ids,"UNKNOWN_CLAIM")
213    if name=="show_evidence":
214        s["shown"]=sorted(set(s["shown"])|selected)
215        return {"evidence":[e for e in evidence_view(ws,path) if e["claim"]["id"] in selected]}
216    if name=="request_review":
217        require(selected<=set(s["shown"]),"SHOW_EVIDENCE_FIRST")
218        require(not selected&set(s["reviewed"]),"ALREADY_REVIEWED")
219        s["pending"]={**args,"candidates":path};s["status"]="awaiting_review"
220        return {"status":"awaiting_human_review","claim_ids":args["claim_ids"],"question":args["question"]}
221    require(selected<=set(s["reviewed"]),"HUMAN_REVIEW_REQUIRED")
222    proposal={**args,"base_hashes":s["base_hashes"],"human_reviews":s["human_reviews"],"deferred_claim_ids":sorted(ids-selected)}
223    s["proposal_revision"]=s.get("proposal_revision",0)+1
224    suffix="" if s["proposal_revision"]==1 else "-"+str(s["proposal_revision"])
225    s["proposal_path"]=s["folder"]+"/proposal"+suffix+".json"
226    ws.write(s["proposal_path"],proposal)
227    ws.write(s["folder"]+"/proposed_business_model"+suffix+".md",args["business_model"].encode(),raw=True)
228    s["proposal"]=proposal;s["proposal_hash"]=digest(ws.raw(s["proposal_path"]));s["status"]="awaiting_apply"
229    return {"status":"awaiting_human_confirmation","proposal":s["proposal_path"],"summary":args["summary"]}
230

真实调用与留存

platform/fde_product/extraction_workflow.py · 本次运行的源码快照

232def advance(ws,path,model,extractor):
233    s=load_session(ws,path)
234    for adapter in (model,extractor):
235        if hasattr(adapter,"deadline"):adapter.deadline=time.monotonic()+600
236    if can_resume(s):
237        # The failed step stays in calls and keeps its files; it does not count against max_steps.
238        s.setdefault("resumed_failures",[]).append({"step":s["calls"][-1]["step"],"error":s["error"],"at":datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()})
239        s["status"]="active";s["error"]=None;save(ws,path,s)
240    require(s["status"]=="active","HUMAN_ACTION_REQUIRED",s["status"])
241    private_path=".runtime/w02-workflow/"+PurePosixPath(s["folder"]).name+".json"
242    private=ws.load(private_path) if ws.path(private_path).exists() else {}
243    specs_by_name={x["function"]["name"]:x["function"]["parameters"] for x in schemas()}
244    try:
245        while s["steps"]-len(s.get("resumed_failures",[]))<s["task"]["max_steps"] and s["status"]=="active":
246            s["steps"]+=1
247            prefix=s["folder"]+f"/model/step-{s['steps']:03}"
248            entry={"step":s["steps"],"mode":model.mode,"request":None,"response":None};s["calls"].append(entry)
249            def record(payload):
250                entry["request"]=ws.write(prefix+".request.json",payload)
251                save(ws,path,s)
252            model.record_request=record
253            if model.mode!="live": record({"messages":deepcopy(s["messages"]),"tools":schemas(),"mode":model.mode})
254            internal=deepcopy(s["messages"])
255            for index,reasoning in private.items():
256                if int(index)<len(internal):internal[int(index)]["reasoning_content"]=reasoning
257            try:
258                message,usage=model.complete(internal,schemas())
259            finally:
260                if getattr(model,"last_call",{}):entry["attempts"]=ws.write(prefix+".attempts.json",model.last_call)
261            if isinstance(message.get("reasoning_content"),str):
262                private[str(len(s["messages"]))]=message["reasoning_content"]
263                save(ws,private_path,private)
264                os.chmod(ws.path(private_path),0o600)
265            require(entry["request"] is not None,"REQUEST_NOT_RECORDED")
266            public={k:deepcopy(message[k]) for k in ("role","content","tool_calls") if k in message}
267            entry["response"]=ws.write(prefix+".response.json",{"message":public,"usage":{k:v for k,v in usage.items() if k in ("prompt_tokens","completion_tokens","total_tokens")}})
268            require(public.get("role")=="assistant","MODEL_INVALID_RESPONSE")
269            calls=public.get("tool_calls")
270            s["messages"].append(public)
271            if not calls:
272                raise CheckError("MODEL_STOPPED_WITHOUT_CHECKPOINT")
273            require(isinstance(calls,list) and 1<=len(calls)<=8,"TOOL_CALLS_INVALID")
274            call_ids=[c.get("id") for c in calls if isinstance(c,dict)]
275            require(len(call_ids)==len(calls) and all(isinstance(i,str) and i for i in call_ids) and len(set(call_ids))==len(call_ids),"TOOL_CALLS_INVALID")
276            save(ws,path,s)
277            for i,call in enumerate(calls):
278                name=call.get("function",{}).get("name","")
279                try:
280                    require(i==0 and s["status"]=="active","ONE_TOOL_AT_A_TIME")
281                    require(name in specs_by_name,"TOOL_FORBIDDEN")
282                    args=parse_json(call["function"]["arguments"]);validate(specs_by_name[name],args)
283                    result=tool(ws,s,name,args,extractor)
284                except (CheckError,KeyError,TypeError) as exc:
285                    result={"status":"error","code":getattr(exc,"code","INVALID_ARGUMENTS")}
286                    if name=="extract_candidates" and s["extraction_run"]:
287                        s["status"]="failed";s["error"]=result["code"]
288                s["tool_events"].append({"step":s["steps"],"name":name,"call_id":call["id"],"result":result})
289                s["messages"].append({"role":"tool","tool_call_id":call["id"],"content":json.dumps(result,ensure_ascii=False)})
290                save(ws,path,s)
291        if s["status"]=="active": raise CheckError("STEP_LIMIT")
292    except (CheckError,ModelError,OSError,UnicodeError) as exc:
293        s["status"]="failed";s["error"]=getattr(exc,"code","FILE_OPERATION_FAILED")
294    finally: save(ws,path,s)
295    return s
296

确认后写入

platform/fde_product/extraction_workflow.py · 本次运行的源码快照

316def apply(ws,path):
317    s=load_session(ws,path)
318    if s["status"]=="completed": return s
319    require(s["status"] in ("awaiting_apply","applying"),"NO_PENDING_PROPOSAL")
320    require(digest(ws.raw(s.get("proposal_path",s["folder"]+"/proposal.json")))==s["proposal_hash"],"PROPOSAL_CHANGED")
321    proposal=ws.load(s.get("proposal_path",s["folder"]+"/proposal.json"))
322    require(proposal==s["proposal"],"PROPOSAL_CHANGED")
323    if s["status"]=="awaiting_apply":
324        for target,expected in s["base_hashes"].items(): require(digest(ws.raw(target))==expected,"MODEL_CHANGED_SINCE_START",target)
325        notes=text(ws,s["snapshots"]["notes"]["path"]).rstrip()+"\n\n## 本轮提取与核查\n\n"+proposal["notes_update"]+"\n\n### FDE 核查反馈\n\n"
326        notes+="\n\n".join("、".join(r["claim_ids"])+"："+r["feedback"] for r in s["human_reviews"])
327        notes+="\n\n未纳入本轮模型草稿的候选："+"、".join(proposal.get("deferred_claim_ids",[]))+"。\n" if proposal.get("deferred_claim_ids") else ""
328        notes+="\n\n### 下一轮起点\n\n"+"\n".join("- "+q for q in proposal["next_questions"])+"\n\n运行依据：`"+s["extraction_run"]+"`。\n"
329        contents={s["task"]["business_model"]:proposal["business_model"].rstrip()+"\n",s["task"]["notes"]:notes}
330        s["apply_files"]=[]
331        for i,(target,content) in enumerate(contents.items()):
332            ref=ws.write(s["folder"]+f"/approved-{i}.md",content.encode(),raw=True)
333            s["apply_files"].append({"target":target,"before":s["base_hashes"][target],"after":ref})
334        s["status"]="applying";s["confirmation"]={"origin":"human_cli","at":datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),"proposal_hash":s["proposal_hash"]}
335        save(ws,path,s)
336    # Replay-safe local transaction. Interrupted writes can finish; external edits are never overwritten.
337    for item in s["apply_files"]:
338        current=digest(ws.raw(item["target"]))
339        require(current in (item["before"],item["after"]["sha256"]),"DOCUMENT_CHANGED_DURING_APPLY",item["target"])
340        require(ws.ref(item["after"]["path"])==item["after"],"HASH_MISMATCH")
341    for item in s["apply_files"]:
342        if digest(ws.raw(item["target"]))==item["after"]["sha256"]: continue
343        require(digest(ws.raw(item["target"]))==item["before"],"DOCUMENT_CHANGED_DURING_APPLY")
344        target=ws.path(item["target"]);tmp=target.with_name(target.name+".workflow-pending")
345        require(not tmp.exists(),"PENDING_WRITE_EXISTS")
346        tmp.write_bytes(ws.raw(item["after"]["path"]));os.replace(tmp,target)
347    s["status"]="completed";save(ws,path,s)
348    return s

351RESUME_HINT = "模型这一步没有回答，对话记录和已给的核查都在。恢复网络或服务后直接 resume，不用改文件。"
352RESTART_HINT = "本轮未完成，不能 resume，也不能 apply。可用 report 查看记录；核对错误后调整，重新 start。"
